<issue_description>
Allow the exporting of grades per assignment id
The main issue I have with the exporting of grades (csv export) is that it is extremely sluggish exporting the entire gradebook (5min+), especially in cases where there may only be one or two grade changes for a given assignment.

My suggestion is to allow the exporting of grades to individual files per assignment id, if an assignment id is specified, otherwise export the entire gradebook.

A custom plugin can be created to achieve this, but I think it may be better to support this.
@jhamrick what do you think?

This is also something that is specific to the use case of a large number of students.
</issue_description>

## Phase 1: READING - Understanding the Problem<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Let me break down the issue description:

1. The main problem is that exporting grades (CSV export) is extremely slow (5+ minutes) when exporting the entire gradebook

Key technical details:
- CSV export functionality needs modification
- Need to support assignment ID filtering
- Should be a core feature, not just a custom plugin
- Performance optimization for large student datasets

- Minimal changes to existing functionality
- Backward compatibility (default behavior unchanged)
- Clear API/interface for specifying assignment ID
- Proper error handling for invalid assignment IDs</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Your thought has been logged.</tool_response><|assistant|></think>### 1.3 Problem Explanation
The issue is about performance optimization for grade export functionality. Currently, nbgrader exports the entire gradebook as CSV, which takes 5+ minutes for large courses. Users want the ability to export grades for specific assignments only, which would significantly improve performance when only a subset of data is needed.

### 1.4 Steps to Reproduce
1. Have a large gradebook with many students and assignments
2. Attempt to export grades via CSV export
3. Observe the slow performance (5+ minutes)
4. Notice that even small changes require exporting the entire dataset

### 1.5 Best Practices
- Maintain backward compatibility - existing export behavior should remain unchanged
- Add clear interface for specifying assignment ID
- Handle invalid assignment IDs gracefully
- Ensure proper testing of both filtered and full exports

    14	
    15	### Instructor toolbar extension for Jupyter notebooks
    16	The nbgrader toolbar extension for Jupyter notebooks guides the instructor through
    17	assignment and grading tasks using the familiar Jupyter notebook interface.
    18	
    19	![Creating assignment](nbgrader/docs/source/user_guide/images/creating_assignment.gif "Creating assignment")
    20	
    21	### Instructor "formgrader" extension for Jupyter notebooks
    22	
    23	The formgrader extension for the Jupyter notebook allows instructors to use
    24	the core functionality of nbgrader---generating the student version of an
    25	assignment, releasing assignments to students, collecting assignments,
    26	autograding submissions, and manually grading submissions.
    27	
    28	![Formgrader extension](nbgrader/docs/source/user_guide/images/formgrader.gif "Formgrader extension")
    29	
    30	### Student assignment list extension for Jupyter notebooks
    31	Using the assignment list extension, students may conveniently view, fetch,
    32	submit, and validate their assignments.
    33	
    34	![nbgrader assignment list](nbgrader/docs/source/user_guide/images/student_assignment.gif "nbgrader assignment list")
    35	
    36	### The command line tools of nbgrader
    37	
    38	[Command line tools](https://nbgrader.readthedocs.io/en/latest/command_line_tools/index.html)
    39	offer an efficient way for the instructor to generate, assign, release, collect,
    40	and grade notebooks.
    41

     2	
     3	from traitlets import Type, Instance, default
     4	from .baseapp import NbGrader
     5	from ..plugins import ExportPlugin, CsvExportPlugin
     6	from ..api import Gradebook
     7	
     8	aliases = {
     9	    'log-level' : 'Application.log_level',
    10	    'db': 'CourseDirectory.db_url',
    11	    'to' : 'ExportPlugin.to',
    12	    'exporter': 'ExportApp.plugin_class'
    13	}
    14	flags = {}
    15	
    16	
    17	class ExportApp(NbGrader):
    18	
    19	    name = u'nbgrader-export'
    20	    description = u'Export information from the database to another format.'
    21	
    22	    aliases = aliases
    23	    flags = flags
    24	
    25	    examples = """
    26	
    27	        The default is to export to a file called "grades.csv", i.e.:
    28	
    29	            nbgrader export
    30	
    31	        You can customize the filename with the --to flag:
    32	
    33	            nbgrader export --to mygrades.csv
    34	
    35	        To change the export type, you will need a class that inherits from
    36	        nbgrader.plugins.ExportPlugin. If your exporter is named
    37	        `MyCustomExporter` and is saved in the file `myexporter.py`, then:
    38	
    39	            nbgrader export --exporter=myexporter.MyCustomExporter
    40	
    41	        """
    42	
    43	    plugin_class = Type(
    44	        CsvExportPlugin,
    45	        klass=ExportPlugin,
    46	        help="The plugin class for exporting the grades."
    47	    ).tag(config=True)
    48	
    49	    plugin_inst = Instance(ExportPlugin).tag(config=False)
    50	
    51	    def init_plugin(self):
    52	        self.log.info("Using exporter: %s", self.plugin_class.__name__)
    53	        self.plugin_inst = self.plugin_class(parent=self)
    54	
    55	    @default("classes")
    56	    def _classes_default(self):
    57	        classes = super(ExportApp, self)._classes_default()
    58	        classes.append(ExportApp)
    59	        classes.append(ExportPlugin)
    60	        return classes
    61	
    62	    def start(self):
    63	        super(ExportApp, self).start()
    64	        self.init_plugin()
    65	        with Gradebook(self.coursedir.db_url) as gb:
    66	            self.plugin_inst.export(gb)

     1	from traitlets import Unicode
     2	
     3	from .base import BasePlugin
     4	from ..api import MissingEntry
     5	
     6	
     7	class ExportPlugin(BasePlugin):
     8	    """Base class for export plugins."""
     9	
    10	    to = Unicode("", help="destination to export to").tag(config=True)
    11	
    12	    def export(self, gradebook):
    13	        """Export grades to another format.
    14	
    15	        This method MUST be implemented by subclasses. Users should be able to
    16	        pass the ``--to`` flag on the command line, which will set the
    17	        ``self.to`` variable. By default, this variable will be an empty string,
    18	        which allows you to specify whatever default you would like.
    19	
    20	        Arguments
    21	        ---------
    22	        gradebook: :class:`nbgrader.api.Gradebook`
    23	            An instance of the gradebook
    24	
    25	        """
    26	        raise NotImplementedError
    27	
    28	
    29	class CsvExportPlugin(ExportPlugin):
    30	    """CSV exporter plugin."""
    31	
    32	    def export(self, gradebook):
    33	        if self.to == "":
    34	            dest = "grades.csv"
    35	        else:
    36	            dest = self.to
    37	
    38	        self.log.info("Exporting grades to %s", dest)
    39	
    40	        fh = open(dest, "w")
    41	        keys = [
    42	            "assignment",
    43	            "duedate",
    44	            "timestamp",
    45	            "student_id",
    46	            "last_name",
    47	            "first_name",
    48	            "email",
    49	            "raw_score",
    50	            "late_submission_penalty",
    51	            "score",
    52	            "max_score"
    53	        ]
    54	        fh.write(",".join(keys) + "\n")
    55	        fmt = ",".join(["{" + x + "}" for x in keys]) + "\n"
    56	
    57	        # Loop over each assignment in the database
    58	        for assignment in gradebook.assignments:
    59	
    60	            # Loop over each student in the database
    61	            for student in gradebook.students:
    62	
    63	                # Create a dictionary that will store information about this
    64	                # student's submitted assignment
    65	                score = {}
    66	                score['assignment'] = assignment.name
    67	                score['duedate'] = assignment.duedate
    68	                score['student_id'] = student.id
    69	                score['last_name'] = student.last_name
    70	                score['first_name'] = student.first_name
    71	                score['email'] = student.email
    72	                score['max_score'] = assignment.max_score
    73	
    74	                # Try to find the submission in the database. If it doesn't
    75	                # exist, the `MissingEntry` exception will be raised, which
    76	                # means the student didn't submit anything, so we assign them a
    77	                # score of zero.
    78	                try:
    79	                    submission = gradebook.find_submission(
    80	                        assignment.name, student.id)
    81	                except MissingEntry:
    82	                    score['timestamp'] = ''
    83	                    score['raw_score'] = 0.0
    84	                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = 0.0
    85	                    score['score'] = 0.0
    86	                else:
    87	                    penalty = submission.late_submission_penalty
    88	                    score['timestamp'] = submission.timestamp
    89	                    score['raw_score'] = submission.score
    90	                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = penalty
    91	                    score['score'] = max(0.0, submission.score - penalty)
    92	
    93	                for key in score:

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.apps.nbgraderapp import NbGraderApp
from nbgrader.api import Gradebook


def setup_test_environment():
    """Set up a test environment with assignments and students."""
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    # Create nbgrader config
    with open(os.path.join(temp_dir, "nbgrader_config.py"), "w") as f:
        f.write("""
c = get_config()
c.CourseDirectory.root = '{}'
c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
    dict(name='ps1'),
    dict(name='ps2')
]
c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [
    dict(id="student1"),
    dict(id="student2")
]
""".format(course_dir))
    
    return temp_dir, course_dir, original_cwd


def create_test_assignments(course_dir):
    """Create some test assignments with notebooks."""
    # Create source directories
    ps1_dir = os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1")
    ps2_dir = os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2")
    os.makedirs(ps1_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(ps2_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Create simple notebook files
    notebook_content = '''{
 "cells": [
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "# Your code here\\n",
    "print(\\"Hello World\\")"
   ]
  }
 ],
 "metadata": {
  "kernelspec": {
   "display_name": "Python 3",
   "language": "python",
   "name": "python3"
  },
  "nbgrader": {
   "assignment_id": "ps1"
  }
 },
 "nbformat": 4,
 "nbformat_minor": 4
}'''
    
    with open(os.path.join(ps1_dir, "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
        f.write(notebook_content.replace('"assignment_id": "ps1"', '"assignment_id": "ps1"'))
        
    with open(os.path.join(ps2_dir, "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
        f.write(notebook_content.replace('"assignment_id": "ps1"', '"assignment_id": "ps2"'))


def run_command(args):
    """Run nbgrader command."""
    print(f"Running: {' '.join(args)}")
    app = NbGraderApp.instance()
    app.initialize(args)
    app.start()


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    temp_dir, course_dir, original_cwd = setup_test_environment()
    
    try:
        # Create assignments
        create_test_assignments(course_dir)
        
        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"
        
        # Run assign commands
        run_command(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_command(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_command(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                print(f"Content preview:\n{content[:500]}...")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)


if __name__ == "__main__":

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.apps.nbgraderapp import NbGraderApp
from nbgrader.api import Gradebook


def setup_test_environment():
    """Set up a test environment with assignments and students."""
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    # Create nbgrader config
    with open(os.path.join(temp_dir, "nbgrader_config.py"), "w") as f:
        f.write("""
c = get_config()
c.CourseDirectory.root = '{}'
c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
    dict(name='ps1'),
    dict(name='ps2')
]
c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [
    dict(id="student1"),
    dict(id="student2")
]
""".format(course_dir))
    
    return temp_dir, course_dir, original_cwd


def create_test_assignments(course_dir):
    """Create some test assignments with notebooks."""
    # Create source directories
    ps1_dir = os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1")
    ps2_dir = os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2")
    os.makedirs(ps1_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(ps2_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Create simple notebook files
    notebook_content = '''{
 "cells": [
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "# Your code here\\n",
    "print(\\"Hello World\\")"
   ]
  }
 ],
 "metadata": {
  "kernelspec": {
   "display_name": "Python 3",
   "language": "python",
   "name": "python3"
  },
  "nbgrader": {
   "assignment_id": "ps1"
  }
 },
 "nbformat": 4,
 "nbformat_minor": 4
}'''
    
    with open(os.path.join(ps1_dir, "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
        f.write(notebook_content.replace('"assignment_id": "ps1"', '"assignment_id": "ps1"'))
        
    with open(os.path.join(ps2_dir, "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
        f.write(notebook_content.replace('"assignment_id": "ps1"', '"assignment_id": "ps2"'))


def run_command(args):
    """Run nbgrader command."""
    print(f"Running: {' '.join(args)}")
    app = NbGraderApp.instance()
    app.initialize(args)
    app.start()


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    temp_dir, course_dir, original_cwd = setup_test_environment()
    
    try:
        # Create assignments
        create_test_assignments(course_dir)
        
        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"
        
        # Run assign commands
        run_command(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_command(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_command(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                print(f"Content preview:\n{content[:500]}...")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality using existing test patterns."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1'),
                dict(name='ps2')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="student1"), dict(id="student2")]""")

        # Create source notebooks
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2"), exist_ok=True)
        
        # Simple notebook content
        notebook_content = '''{
 "cells": [
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "print(\\"Hello World\\")"
   ]
  }
 ],
 "metadata": {
  "kernelspec": {
   "display_name": "Python 3",
   "language": "python",
   "name": "python3"
  }
 },
 "nbformat": 4,
 "nbformat_minor": 4
}'''

        with open(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
            f.write(notebook_content)
            
        with open(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
            f.write(notebook_content)

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                lines = content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"Number of rows: {len(lines)}")
                print(f"Header: {lines[0]}")
                if len(lines) > 1:
                    print(f"First data row: {lines[1]}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality using existing test patterns."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1'),
                dict(name='ps2')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="student1"), dict(id="student2")]""")

        # Create source notebooks
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2"), exist_ok=True)
        
        # Simple notebook content
        notebook_content = '''{
 "cells": [
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "print(\\"Hello World\\")"
   ]
  }
 ],
 "metadata": {
  "kernelspec": {
   "display_name": "Python 3",
   "language": "python",
   "name": "python3"
  }
 },
 "nbformat": 4,
 "nbformat_minor": 4
}'''

        with open(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
            f.write(notebook_content)
            
        with open(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "problem1.ipynb"), "w") as f:
            f.write(notebook_content)

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                lines = content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"Number of rows: {len(lines)}")
                print(f"Header: {lines[0]}")
                if len(lines) > 1:
                    print(f"First data row: {lines[1]}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality using existing test patterns."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1'),
                dict(name='ps2')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="student1"), dict(id="student2")]""")

        # Create source notebooks by copying test files
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2"), exist_ok=True)
        
        # Copy existing test notebooks
        from shutil import copyfile
        test_files_dir = os.path.join(original_cwd, "nbgrader", "tests", "apps", "files")
        copyfile(
            os.path.join(test_files_dir, "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"),
            os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "problem1.ipynb")
        )
        copyfile(
            os.path.join(test_files_dir, "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"),
            os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "problem1.ipynb")
        )

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                lines = content.strip().split('\n')

            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")

        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def _copy_file(src, dest):
    """Copy file helper function."""
    full_src = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "nbgrader", "tests", "apps", src)
    full_dest = os.path.abspath(dest)
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(full_dest)):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(full_dest))
    if os.path.exists(full_dest):
        remove(full_dest)
    shutil.copy(full_src, full_dest)


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality using existing test patterns."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        # Copy test notebooks following the exact same pattern as existing tests
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        
        # Copy submitted notebooks too
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Run autograde commands
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                contents = f.readlines()
                print(f"Number of rows: {len(contents)}")
                print(f"Header: {contents[0].strip()}")
                if len(contents) > 1:
                    print(f"Sample data rows:")
                    for i in range(1, min(3, len(contents))):
                        print(f"  Row {i}: {contents[i].strip()}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Reproduction script to test current export functionality 
and later test assignment-specific export functionality.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def test_current_export():
    """Test the current export functionality using existing test patterns."""
    print("=== Testing Current Export Functionality ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1'),
                dict(name='ps2')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="student1"), dict(id="student2")]""")

        # Create source notebooks by copying test files
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2"), exist_ok=True)
        
        # Copy existing test notebooks
        from shutil import copyfile
        test_files_dir = os.path.join(original_cwd, "nbgrader", "tests", "apps", "files")
        copyfile(
            os.path.join(test_files_dir, "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"),
            os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "problem1.ipynb")
        )
        copyfile(
            os.path.join(test_files_dir, "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"),
            os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "problem1.ipynb")
        )

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                lines = content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"Number of rows: {len(lines)}")
                print(f"Header: {lines[0]}")
                if len(lines) > 1:
                    print(f"First data row: {lines[1]}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

"""
Simple test to examine current export structure
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def _copy_file(src, dest):
    """Copy file helper function."""
    full_src = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "nbgrader", "tests", "apps", src)
    full_dest = os.path.abspath(dest)
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(full_dest)):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(full_dest))
    if os.path.exists(full_dest):
        remove(full_dest)
    shutil.copy(full_src, full_dest)


def examine_export_structure():
    """Examine the current export structure."""
    print("=== Examining Current Export Structure ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        # Copy test notebooks following the exact same pattern as existing tests
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        
        # Copy submitted notebooks too
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Run autograde commands
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Export grades (current behavior - all assignments)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        # Check if grades.csv was created and examine its content
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                content = f.read()
                print("=== Full Content ===")
                print(content)
                
                lines = content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"\n=== Summary ===")
                print(f"Total rows: {len(lines)}")
                print(f"Header: {lines[0]}")
                if len(lines) > 1:
                    print(f"Data rows: {len(lines) - 1}")
                    assignments_in_export = set()
                    for line in lines[1:]:
                        parts = line.split(',')
                        if len(parts) > 0:
                            assignments_in_export.add(parts[0])  # assignment column
                    print(f"Assignments in export: {sorted(assignments_in_export)}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

from .. import run_nbgrader


def debug_export():
    """Debug the export by replicating the test but with inspection."""
    print("=== Debugging Export Structure ===")
    
    # Create test app instance
    test_app = test_module.TestNbGraderExport()
    
    # Create temporary directory like the test framework does
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = temp_dir
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Replicate the exact test setup
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "a") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        # Copy files like the test does
        test_files_dir = os.path.join(original_cwd, "nbgrader", "tests", "apps")
        def _copy_file(src, dest):
            full_src = join(test_files_dir, src)
            full_dest = os.path.abspath(dest)
            if not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(full_dest)):
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(full_dest))
            if os.path.exists(full_dest):
                from nbgrader.utils import remove
                remove(full_dest)
            shutil.copy(full_src, full_dest)
        
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        
        db = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db])

        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db])

        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db])
        
        # Examine the exported file
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            print("✓ grades.csv created successfully")
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as fh:
                contents = fh.readlines()
            print(f"Number of rows: {len(contents)}")
            print("=== CONTENTS ===")
            for i, line in enumerate(contents):
                print(f"{i:2d}: {line.rstrip()}")
                
            print("\n=== SUMMARY ===")
            header = contents[0].strip().split(',')
            print(f"Columns ({len(header)}): {header}")
            
            # Count assignments
            assignments = set()
            for line in contents[1:]:
                parts = line.strip().split(',')
                if len(parts) > 0:
                    assignments.add(parts[0])
            print(f"Unique assignments: {sorted(assignments)}")
            print(f"Students: {[line.split(',')[4] for line in contents[1:]]}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

    75	                # exist, the `MissingEntry` exception will be raised, which
    76	                # means the student didn't submit anything, so we assign them a
    77	                # score of zero.

    79	                    submission = gradebook.find_submission(
    80	                        assignment.name, student.id)

    85	                    score['score'] = 0.0

    89	                    score['raw_score'] = submission.score
    90	                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = penalty
    91	                    score['score'] = max(0.0, submission.score - penalty)

    94	                    if score[key] is None:
    95	                        score[key] = ''
    96	                    if not isinstance(score[key], str):
    97	                        score[key] = str(score[key])

</tool_response><|assistant|></think>Perfect! Now I understand the structure. Looking at the `CsvExportPlugin.export()` method:

1. It loops through **all assignments** (`for assignment in gradebook.assignments`)
2. For each assignment, it loops through **all students** (`for student in gradebook.students`)  
3. It exports data for every combination of assignment and student

The issue is asking to allow exporting grades for **specific assignment IDs only**, instead of all assignments.

    def start(self):
        super(DbAssignmentRemoveApp, self).start()

        if len(self.extra_args) != 1:
            self.fail("No assignment id provided.")

        assignment_id = self.extra_args[0]

        with Gradebook(self.coursedir.db_url) as gb:
            try:
                assignment = gb.find_assignment(assignment_id)
            except MissingEntry:
                self.fail("No such assignment: '%s'", assignment_id)

            if len(assignment.submissions) > 0:
                if self.force:
                    self.log.warning("Removing associated grades")
                else:
                    self.log.warning("!!! There are grades in the database for assignment '%s'.", assignment_id)
                    self.log.warning("!!! Removing this assignment will also remove these grades.")
                    self.fail("!!! If you are SURE this is what you want to do, rerun with --force.")

            self.log.info("Removing assignment with ID '%s'", assignment_id)
            gb.remove_assignment(assignment_id)

## Phase 5: FIX ANALYSIS 

### 5.1 Problem Statement
The current CSV export functionality exports grades for ALL assignments in the gradebook, which is slow (5+ minutes) for large courses. Users want the ability to export grades for specific assignments only.

### 5.2 Problem Location  
The problem is in two files:
1. `/workspace/jupyter__nbgrader__0.5/nbgrader/apps/exportapp.py` - needs a new command-line flag for assignment ID
2. `/workspace/jupyter__nbgrader__0.5/nbgrader/plugins/export.py` - needs modification to CsvExportPlugin to filter by assignment ID

### 5.3 How the Issue Manifests
Currently, the `CsvExportPlugin.export()` method loops through all assignments (`gradebook.assignments`) and all students. For large courses with many assignments, this creates unnecessarily large export files and takes significant time.

### 5.4 Best Practices for the Fix
- Maintain backward compatibility (default behavior unchanged)
- Use the existing traitlets configuration system
- Handle invalid assignment IDs gracefully with proper error messages
- Follow the same pattern as other nbgrader CLI applications
- Allow `None`/empty values for the assignment ID to mean "export all"

### 5.5 Solution Approach
1. Add an `assignment_id` traitlet to `ExportApp` class that defaults to `None`
2. Add the assignment_id to the aliases mapping so `--assignment` maps to this traitlet  
3. Pass the assignment_id to the plugin through configuration
4. Modify `CsvExportPlugin` to accept an `assignment_id` traitlet
5. Update the `export()` method to filter assignments when `assignment_id` is specified
6. Handle the case where the specified assignment ID doesn't exist

</tool_response><|assistant|></think>Now I'll modify the CsvExportPlugin.export method to handle assignment filtering:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/jupyter__nbgrader__0.5/nbgrader/plugins/export.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>class CsvExportPlugin(ExportPlugin):
    """CSV exporter plugin."""

    def export(self, gradebook):
        if self.to == "":
            dest = "grades.csv"
        else:
            dest = self.to

        self.log.info("Exporting grades to %s", dest)
        if self.assignment_id is not None:
            self.log.info("Filtering export to assignment: %s", self.assignment_id)

        fh = open(dest, "w")
        keys = [
            "assignment",
            "duedate",
            "timestamp",
            "student_id",
            "last_name",
            "first_name",
            "email",
            "raw_score",
            "late_submission_penalty",
            "score",
            "max_score"
        ]
        fh.write(",".join(keys) + "\n")
        fmt = ",".join(["{" + x + "}" for x in keys]) + "\n"

        # Determine which assignments to export
        if self.assignment_id is not None:
            # Export only the specified assignment
            try:
                assignment = gradebook.find_assignment(self.assignment_id)
                assignments_to_export = [assignment]
                self.log.info("Found assignment '%s' to export", self.assignment_id)
            except MissingEntry:
                self.log.error("No such assignment: %s", self.assignment_id)
                fh.close()
                raise ValueError(f"No such assignment: {self.assignment_id}")
        else:
            # Export all assignments (current behavior)
            assignments_to_export = gradebook.assignments
            self.log.info("Exporting all %d assignments", len(assignments_to_export))

        # Loop over selected assignments
        for assignment in assignments_to_export:

            # Loop over each student in the database
            for student in gradebook.students:

                # Create a dictionary that will store information about this
                # student's submitted assignment
                score = {}
                score['assignment'] = assignment.name
                score['duedate'] = assignment.duedate
                score['student_id'] = student.id
                score['last_name'] = student.last_name
                score['first_name'] = student.first_name
                score['email'] = student.email
                score['max_score'] = assignment.max_score

                # Try to find the submission in the database. If it doesn't
                # exist, the `MissingEntry` exception will be raised, which
                # means the student didn't submit anything, so we assign them a
                # score of zero.
                try:
                    submission = gradebook.find_submission(
                        assignment.name, student.id)
                except MissingEntry:
                    score['timestamp'] = ''
                    score['raw_score'] = 0.0
                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = 0.0
                    score['score'] = 0.0
                else:
                    penalty = submission.late_submission_penalty
                    score['timestamp'] = submission.timestamp
                    score['raw_score'] = submission.score
                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = penalty
                    score['score'] = max(0.0, submission.score - penalty)

                for key in score:
                    if score[key] is None:
                        score[key] = ''
                    if not isinstance(score[key], str):
                        score[key] = str(score[key])

                fh.write(fmt.format(**score))

        fh.close()</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>class CsvExportPlugin(ExportPlugin):
    """CSV exporter plugin."""

    def export(self, gradebook):
        if self.to == "":
            dest = "grades.csv"
        else:
            dest = self.to

        self.log.info("Exporting grades to %s", dest)

        fh = open(dest, "w")
        keys = [
            "assignment",
            "duedate",
            "timestamp",
            "student_id",
            "last_name",
            "first_name",
            "email",
            "raw_score",
            "late_submission_penalty",
            "score",
            "max_score"
        ]
        fh.write(",".join(keys) + "\n")
        fmt = ",".join(["{" + x + "}" for x in keys]) + "\n"

        # Loop over each assignment in the database
        for assignment in gradebook.assignments:

            # Loop over each student in the database
            for student in gradebook.students:

                # Create a dictionary that will store information about this
                # student's submitted assignment
                score = {}
                score['assignment'] = assignment.name
                score['duedate'] = assignment.duedate
                score['student_id'] = student.id
                score['last_name'] = student.last_name
                score['first_name'] = student.first_name
                score['email'] = student.email
                score['max_score'] = assignment.max_score

                # Try to find the submission in the database. If it doesn't
                # exist, the `MissingEntry` exception will be raised, which
                # means the student didn't submit anything, so we assign them a
                # score of zero.
                try:
                    submission = gradebook.find_submission(
                        assignment.name, student.id)
                except MissingEntry:
                    score['timestamp'] = ''
                    score['raw_score'] = 0.0
                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = 0.0
                    score['score'] = 0.0
                else:
                    penalty = submission.late_submission_penalty
                    score['timestamp'] = submission.timestamp
                    score['raw_score'] = submission.score
                    score['late_submission_penalty'] = penalty
                    score['score'] = max(0.0, submission.score - penalty)

                for key in score:
                    if score[key] is None:
                        score[key] = ''
                    if not isinstance(score[key], str):
                        score[key] = str(score[key])

                fh.write(fmt.format(**score))

        fh.close()</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/jupyter__nbgrader__0.5/nbgrader/plugins/export.py has been edited. """
Test script to verify assignment-specific export functionality
"""

import os
import tempfile
import shutil
from nbgrader.tests import run_nbgrader
from nbgrader.utils import remove


def _copy_file(src, dest):
    """Copy file helper function."""
    full_src = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "nbgrader", "tests", "apps", src)
    full_dest = os.path.abspath(dest)
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(full_dest)):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(full_dest))
    if os.path.exists(full_dest):
        remove(full_dest)
    shutil.copy(full_src, full_dest)


def test_assignment_specific_export():
    """Test exporting grades for a specific assignment."""
    print("=== Testing Assignment-Specific Export ===")
    
    # Create temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    course_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, "course101")
    os.makedirs(course_dir)
    
    # Change to temp directory
    original_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(temp_dir)
    
    try:
        # Create nbgrader config
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "w") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        # Copy test notebooks
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-unchanged.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        
        # Copy submitted notebooks
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        _copy_file("files/submitted-changed.ipynb", os.path.join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))

        # Create database URL
        db_url = f"sqlite:///{os.path.join(temp_dir, 'gradebook.db')}"

        # Run assign commands
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Run autograde commands
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db_url])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db_url])

        # Test 1: Export all assignments (default behavior)
        print("\n--- Test 1: Export all assignments ---")
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url])
        
        if os.path.exists("grades.csv"):
            with open("grades.csv", "r") as f:
                all_content = f.read()
                all_lines = all_content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"✓ All assignments export: {len(all_lines)} rows")
                print(f"  Header: {all_lines[0]}")
                # Count unique assignments
                assignments = set()
                for line in all_lines[1:]:
                    parts = line.split(',')
                    if len(parts) > 0:
                        assignments.add(parts[0])
                print(f"  Assignments found: {sorted(assignments)}")
        else:
            print("✗ grades.csv was not created for all assignments")

        # Test 2: Export only ps1 assignment
        print("\n--- Test 2: Export only ps1 assignment ---")
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url, "--assignment", "ps1", "--to", "ps1_grades.csv"])
        
        if os.path.exists("ps1_grades.csv"):
            with open("ps1_grades.csv", "r") as f:
                ps1_content = f.read()
                ps1_lines = ps1_content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"✓ ps1 assignment export: {len(ps1_lines)} rows")
                print(f"  Header: {ps1_lines[0]}")
                # Verify only ps1 assignments
                ps1_assignments = set()
                for line in ps1_lines[1:]:
                    parts = line.split(',')
                    if len(parts) > 0:
                        ps1_assignments.add(parts[0])
                print(f"  Assignments found: {sorted(ps1_assignments)}")
                if ps1_assignments == {"ps1"}:
                    print("  ✓ Correctly filtered to only ps1")
                else:
                    print(f"  ✗ Expected only 'ps1', got {ps1_assignments}")
        else:
            print("✗ ps1_grades.csv was not created")

        # Test 3: Export only ps2 assignment
        print("\n--- Test 3: Export only ps2 assignment ---")
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url, "--assignment", "ps2", "--to", "ps2_grades.csv"])
        
        if os.path.exists("ps2_grades.csv"):
            with open("ps2_grades.csv", "r") as f:
                ps2_content = f.read()
                ps2_lines = ps2_content.strip().split('\n')
                print(f"✓ ps2 assignment export: {len(ps2_lines)} rows")
                print(f"  Header: {ps2_lines[0]}")
                # Verify only ps2 assignments
                ps2_assignments = set()
                for line in ps2_lines[1:]:
                    parts = line.split(',')
                    if len(parts) > 0:
                        ps2_assignments.add(parts[0])
                print(f"  Assignments found: {sorted(ps2_assignments)}")
                if ps2_assignments == {"ps2"}:
                    print("  ✓ Correctly filtered to only ps2")
                else:
                    print(f"  ✗ Expected only 'ps2', got {ps2_assignments}")
        else:
            print("✗ ps2_grades.csv was not created")

        # Test 4: Try to export non-existent assignment (should fail)
        print("\n--- Test 4: Export non-existent assignment ---")
        try:
            run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db_url, "--assignment", "nonexistent", "--to", "fail.csv"])
            print("✗ Should have failed for non-existent assignment")
        except AssertionError:
            print("✓ Correctly failed for non-existent assignment")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"? Failed with unexpected error: {e}")

        # Summary
        print("\n=== SUMMARY ===")
        print("Expected behavior:")
        print("- Export without --assignment flag exports all assignments")
        print("- Export with --assignment flag exports only that assignment")
        print("- Export with invalid assignment fails appropriately")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during test: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        # Cleanup
        os.chdir(original_cwd)
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)

Class parameters
----------------

Parameters are set from command-line arguments of the form:
--
    Default: '.nbgrader.log'
    Name of the logfile to log to.

CourseDirectory options
-----------------------
--CourseDirectory.assignment_id=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
    The assignment name. This MUST be specified, either by setting the config
    option, passing an argument on the command line, or using the --assignment
    option on the command line.
--CourseDirectory.autograded_directory=<Unicode>
    Default: 'autograded'
    The name of the directory that contains assignment submissions after they
    have been autograded. This corresponds to the `nbgrader_step` variable in
    the `directory_structure` config option.
--CourseDirectory.db_assignments=<List>
    Default: []
    A list of assignments that will be created in the database. Each item in the
    list should be a dictionary with the following keys:
        - name
        - duedate (optional)
    The values will be stored in the database. Please see the API documentation
    on the `Assignment` database model for details on these fields.
--
--CourseDirectory.db_url=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
    URL to the database. Defaults to sqlite:///<root>/gradebook.db, where <root>
    is another configurable variable.
--CourseDirectory.directory_structure=<Unicode>
    Default: '{nbgrader_step}/{student_id}/{assignment_id}'
    Format string for the directory structure that nbgrader works over during
    the grading process. This MUST contain named keys for 'nbgrader_step',
    'student_id', and 'assignment_id'. It SHOULD NOT contain a key for
    'notebook_id', as this will be automatically joined with the rest of the
    path.
--CourseDirectory.feedback_directory=<Unicode>
    Default: 'feedback'
    The name of the directory that contains assignment feedback after grading
    has been completed. This corresponds to the `nbgrader_step` variable in the
    `directory_structure` config option.
--CourseDirectory.ignore=<List>
    Default: ['.ipynb_checkpoints', '*.pyc', '__pycache__']
    List of file names or file globs to be ignored when copying directories.
--
    Default: '*'
    File glob to match notebook names, excluding the '.ipynb' extension. This
    can be changed to filter by notebook.
--CourseDirectory.release_directory=<Unicode>
    Default: 'release'
    The name of the directory that contains the version of the assignment that
    will be released to students. This corresponds to the `nbgrader_step`
    variable in the `directory_structure` config option.
--CourseDirectory.root=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
    The root directory for the course files (that includes the `source`,
    `release`, `submitted`, `autograded`, etc. directories). Defaults to the
    current working directory.
--CourseDirectory.source_directory=<Unicode>
    Default: 'source'
    The name of the directory that contains the master/instructor version of
    assignments. This corresponds to the `nbgrader_step` variable in the
    `directory_structure` config option.
--CourseDirectory.student_id=<Unicode>
    Default: '*'
    File glob to match student IDs. This can be changed to filter by student.
    Note: this is always changed to '.' when running `nbgrader assign`, as the
--
    string, for example `--student=""12345""`. See:
        https://github.com/jupyter/nbgrader/issues/743
    for more details.
--CourseDirectory.submitted_directory=<Unicode>
    Default: 'submitted'
    The name of the directory that contains assignments that have been submitted
    by students for grading. This corresponds to the `nbgrader_step` variable in
    the `directory_structure` config option.

ExportApp options
-----------------
--ExportApp.answer_yes=<Bool>
    Default: False
    Answer yes to any prompts.
--ExportApp.assignment_id=<Unicode>
    Default: None
    The assignment ID to export grades for. If not specified, exports all
    assignments.
--ExportApp.config_file=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
    Full path of a config file.
--ExportApp.config_file_name=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
--
    Default: 'nbgrader.plugins.export.CsvExportPlugin'
    The plugin class for exporting the grades.

ExportPlugin options
--------------------
--ExportPlugin.assignment_id=<Unicode>
    Default: None
    assignment ID to export (if None, export all)
--ExportPlugin.to=<Unicode>
    Default: ''
    destination to export to

Examples
--

    You can customize the filename with the --to flag:

        nbgrader export --to mygrades.csv

    To export grades for a specific assignment only:

        nbgrader export --assignment ps1

    To change the export type, you will need a class that inherits from
    nbgrader.plugins.ExportPlugin. If your exporter is named
    `MyCustomExporter` and is saved in the file `myexporter.py`, then:

from os.path import join
from ...utils import remove
from .. import run_nbgrader
from .base import BaseTestApp


class TestNbGraderExport(BaseTestApp):

    def test_help(self):
        """Does the help display without error?"""
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--help-all"])

    def test_export(self, db, course_dir):
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "a") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db])

        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-changed.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-changed.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db])

        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db])
        assert os.path.isfile("grades.csv")
        with open("grades.csv", "r") as fh:
            contents = fh.readlines()
        assert len(contents) == 5

        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--to", "mygrades.csv"])
        assert os.path.isfile("mygrades.csv")

        remove("grades.csv")
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--exporter", "nbgrader.plugins.CsvExportPlugin"])
        assert os.path.isfile("grades.csv")

        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--exporter=nbgrader.tests.apps.files.myexporter.MyExporter", "--to", "foo.txt"])
        assert os.path.isfile("foo.txt")

    def test_export_assignment_filter(self, db, course_dir):
        """Test exporting grades for a specific assignment only."""
        with open("nbgrader_config.py", "a") as fh:
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_assignments = [
                dict(name='ps1', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles'),
                dict(name='ps2', duedate='2015-02-02 14:58:23.948203 America/Los_Angeles')]\n""")
            fh.write("""c.CourseDirectory.db_students = [dict(id="foo"), dict(id="bar")]""")

        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "source", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-unchanged.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "source", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["assign", "ps2", "--db", db])

        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-changed.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "submitted", "bar", "ps1", "p1.ipynb"))
        self._copy_file(join("files", "submitted-changed.ipynb"), join(course_dir, "submitted", "foo", "ps2", "p1.ipynb"))
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps1", "--db", db])
        run_nbgrader(["autograde", "ps2", "--db", db])

        # Test 1: Export all assignments (default behavior)
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db])
        assert os.path.isfile("grades.csv")
        with open("grades.csv", "r") as fh:
            all_contents = fh.readlines()
        assert len(all_contents) == 5  # 1 header + 4 data rows (2 assignments × 2 students)

        # Extract assignments from the export
        assignments_in_all = set()
        for line in all_contents[1:]:  # Skip header
            parts = line.strip().split(',')
            if len(parts) > 0:
                assignments_in_all.add(parts[0])
        assert assignments_in_all == {"ps1", "ps2"}

        # Test 2: Export only ps1 assignment
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--assignment", "ps1", "--to", "ps1_grades.csv"])
        assert os.path.isfile("ps1_grades.csv")
        with open("ps1_grades.csv", "r") as fh:
            ps1_contents = fh.readlines()
        assert len(ps1_contents) == 3  # 1 header + 2 data rows (1 assignment × 2 students)

        # Verify only ps1 assignments in the filtered export
        assignments_in_ps1 = set()
        for line in ps1_contents[1:]:  # Skip header
            parts = line.strip().split(',')
            if len(parts) > 0:
                assignments_in_ps1.add(parts[0])
        assert assignments_in_ps1 == {"ps1"}

        # Test 3: Export only ps2 assignment
        run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--assignment", "ps2", "--to", "ps2_grades.csv"])
        assert os.path.isfile("ps2_grades.csv")
        with open("ps2_grades.csv", "r") as fh:
            ps2_contents = fh.readlines()
        assert len(ps2_contents) == 3  # 1 header + 2 data rows (1 assignment × 2 students)

        # Verify only ps2 assignments in the filtered export
        assignments_in_ps2 = set()
        for line in ps2_contents[1:]:  # Skip header
            parts = line.strip().split(',')
            if len(parts) > 0:
                assignments_in_ps2.add(parts[0])
        assert assignments_in_ps2 == {"ps2"}

        # Test 4: Try to export non-existent assignment (should fail)
        failed = False
        try:
            run_nbgrader(["export", "--db", db, "--assignment", "nonexistent", "--to", "fail.csv"])
        except AssertionError:
            # This is expected - the test framework catches the error
            failed = True
        assert failed, "Should have failed for non-existent assignment"</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>import os

        Raises ``sqlalchemy.orm.exc.NoResultFound`` if the query selects
